# ⛔ CONTINUITY EDITOR RULINGS (binding, OVERRIDE the cards above)

## 1. Villain arc / state — NOT clean

- **S0 × S1 — the birth is staged twice, and the two cards declare opposite chronologies. RULING: S0 precedes S1** (the sticker gun is holstered on the hero's belt in S0 and S1 is where the copy takes it off him; S1-first is physically impossible). Therefore:
  - **S1** — strike the "CHRONOLOGY, declared and binding: S1 is past tense, the receipt, story-time earlier than S0" paragraph, and strike the escalation "S0 must lose the villain's BODY / a slate nub and one shades-glint" (a glint in S0 pre-spends S1's f40 shades-resolve). S1's keys-off-the-S0-belt demand is already satisfied — delete it too.
  - **S0** — cut f118 ("cast shadow detaches at the shoulder"), f120 ("steps forward… stands up") and gag 3 "THE SHADOW THAT STANDS UP." The silhouette is simply **already in the dead unlit corner from f96**, with the rain, never shown separating. It hauls, locks, leaves. One peel, one birth, and it is S1's.
- **S3 × S4 — S3 reloads the weapon S4 needs dead.** Cut S3's f222 "re-seats a spare die off the bandolier" beat (keep the bandolier as texture). The gun is broken from S3 f188 and never works again.
- **S4 × S5 — dead gun vs firing gun.** Strike from S5: "it fires with a hitch" and the f168/f222 gun CLUNK-as-firing. Per S4 and S6 he **cannot issue**: he hand-peels a sticker off the spool and presents it on the tip of a dead gun. Both S5 cons use the hand-peel.
- **S1 / S3 / S4 / S5 / S6 — four different damage states.** Lock ONE, carried on `<Villain/>` from S3 f188 to S7's confiscation: **head/die split open (the S3 shatter), spring out of the crack, barrel wire-bound and taped, ink pad dried and cracked, brass flip-counter jammed.** Delete S1's ledger line "a hairline CRACK, not a shatter" (S3 ships the shatter and its shards are S3's payoff prop).
- **S6 × S4/S5/S8 — "the last note it ever printed, carried crumpled for three scenes" is false.** He has the spool (S4 f58, S5 "roll back in his jacket", S8's roll spilling from the split). Strike the "last note" pathos; the item he smooths and feeds the slot is **one sticker off the same roll**.
- **S6 × S1/S4/S5/S8 — the gun is not a note printer.** Strike "receipt printer that RATCHETS OUT banknote-proportioned DONE slips" and "the bin heaps with crumpled notes." It is S1's tagger with a spool of die-cut `#A8B84A` DONE stickers; the reject bin heaps with **crumpled identical DONE stickers**. Keep the bill-validator gag unchanged.
- **S3 × S4/S5/S6/S7 — the collar ink dies after S3.** S3's hook promises "he wears his own lie into S4." Add the yellow-green stain on the white collar as a permanent `<Villain/>` scene-override in **S4, S5, S6 and S7**.
- **S4 × S5 — the red flag has no origin.** S4's flag is rammed into the **mast** at f68–74 and is still oscillating at f104; it is never clipped to the villain. **Cut S5's shoulder flag and gag 10.** Replace S5's f204 grooming beat with him dabbing/straightening the S3 collar ink (see above) — which also de-duplicates S7's flag gag (§4).
- **S5 — "his mouth appears for the first time in the reel" is false.** S1 f142, S3 f188 and S4 f62 all precede it. Strike "for the first time in the reel"; also strike "and it bends" (the bible and S5's own sprite block say flat line only).
- **S2 — code-rain breaches the component floor.** The bible spec (cited verbatim in S6 and S7) is **3 to 5 columns**. S2's fix cut it to two. Restore to **3 tight columns**, x≈590–660, so the queue's real estate is unaffected.
- **S6 × S7 — the lanyard is not planted.** S7 requires "the black lanyard and the laminated licence are planted on him no later than S6 and carried into S7 unchanged." **Add the black cord + laminated licence to S6's villain sprite** (same laminate stock / corner radius / foil chip as the hero's card).
- **S6 × S7 — "He is not a forger" contradicts the scene before it.** S6 catches him feeding a forgery into a counterfeit detector and leaves him standing in a bin of them. **S7: strike "The Claude standing there is not a forger" and "an honest gauge."** The gauge is honest; he is not. The "it could happen to anyone" read must come from the machine's indifference (it does not know who he is), not from claiming his record is clean. His 20 gold holes stay — they are machine-lit, which is exactly the point.
- **S7 × S8 — confiscated vs crushed, and an impossible tread.** S7's fix 21 rules the gun is **confiscated** (swallowed sideways by the validator throat) — no press, no crush. S8's "crushed dead flat, with the S6 press's tread pattern pressed into its shell" is unbuildable (S6's press never touches it). **Strike the tread and the crush from S8.**
- **S8 × S9 — the gun is in two places.** S8 bins it roadside at f30–38; S9 pins it, snapped, to the authority's corkboard under DO NOT REFIT. **RULING: S7's throat swallowed it into the authority's custody → S9 keeps it.** Cut the gun from S8's skip spill; S8's legible kit = the `#A8B84A` spool streaking in the slipstream + the f34 shades pop.

## 2. Payoff props — mostly clean, four corrections

- **Keys (once, S7) — clean on the handover**, but the plant is disputed: **S0 and S7 both say S3 houses the key cabinet** ("the keys get exactly one home, S3's hook under the wired glass"; "the same cabinet from S3's back wall, finally opening, Chekhov paid off") while **S3 says "the keys are not in this scene at all."** RULING: **S3 plants it.** Add a wired-glass cabinet with a red LOCKED bolt and the ring visible inside — never lit, never approached, never referenced. Strike S3's "not in this scene at all."
- **S3 — strike "S1 already established direct address with the key toss."** S1 bans keys outright. S1's direct address is established by sticker 09 on the lens at f150.
- **L-plate (once, S7) — clean**, but **S2 and S3's handoff lines say "at S7's 20th punch."** S7 tears it at **f126–140**, 34+ frames after the 20th punch (f90–92), in the dawn bar, before the keys. Correct both lines to "at S7, in the dawn bar, before the keys."
- **Multiply (once, S5) — clean.** S1, S2, S4, S6, S7 all explicitly defer; S5 spends it and un-makes it inside its own 256 frames.
- **S1 × S2 — sticker 08 does not carry.** S1 ends with sticker 08 crooked on the hero's chest and S2 opens seconds later (S1's own hook). **Add it to S2's hero sprite** (already inside S2's declared text budget — DONE is on the list).
- **S1's peel reservation is over-broad and is breached three times.** "Every peel, every backing-paper release and every adhesive tear in the entire reel is reserved for f18-f44" is violated by S4's f44–52 hand-peel, S5's f252 peel-onto-the-film and S8's f118 adhesive release, all load-bearing. **Narrow the rule to: the long wet peeling-adhesive TEAR and the body-separation image are S1-only; later releases must be small, dry and short.**
- **S7 × S8 — the envelope is delivered twice.** S7 fires it into the **viewer's** panel tile ("that is what makes it yours"). S8 hands "S7's envelope, the one this scene's IN transition is made of" to the instructor at f60. **Strike the identity claim in S8**: f60 is the **same broadcast landing in a second recipient's tile** (same 95% face, same red (1)), not the same object arriving twice.

## 3. Shared object / set / palette / camera — clean, with two stale reservations

- No two scenes twin badly. S1↔S3 share the forecourt, the wreck, the JIFFY LOOB sign and a low camera **by design** (the transformation read), and diverge on light (fire-orange chaos vs. blue-white floods). S6↔S7 both carry sanctioned DMV dressing but differ on scale, palette (green tile vs. brass/cream) and camera (worm's-eye vs. chest-height). Palette spine holds: `#3F9E74` is issued only by the S6 press and by objects that **are** S6 passes (S8's caps, S9's roundel); S5's total green ban is intact.
- **Stale reservation: the turntable/revolving stage.** S1, S2, S3, S4, S5 and S9 all reserve "the revolve / the turntable" for S8. **S8 has no turntable** — it is the open road with S2's learner car. Strike the revolve from all six collision lists.
- **S2 — the escalation to the board is stale and resolves itself.** S8 ships the car, the empty passenger seat and the unbolted pedal, i.e. exactly the bible's S8 row. Delete S2's "the board's S8 is a revolving turntable with no car" rationale and its ⚠️ escalation. S8's dependency (pedal bolted and functional through S7, no punt) is **already satisfied** — S7 and S3 never touch it.

## 4. Parody reuse — one over-run

- **The tin bot judges the villain twice, in consecutive scenes.** S6 f92 (winds down, points one arm at the bin) and S7 f196 (clips his red flag to the empty lanyard) are the same joke. **Cut S6's point-at-the-bin beat**; the bot stays as an unremarked patrol layer, per S9's demotion. S7 owns "the cheapest thing outlives the smartest." (S5's shoulder flag is cut under §1, which removes the third instance.)
- **S3 × S5 — the JIFFY LOOB payoff line disagrees.** S3's gag 5 mandates "WE DON'T CERTIFY OURSELVES ANYMORE"; S5 ships "WE READ THE ORDER NOW." **S5's set copy is canonical** — correct S3's gag 5 to the shipped string.
- Everything else holds: Matrix rain is the mandated aura, not a recurring bit; Agent Smith multiply once; hydraulic press once (S9's is the board-sanctioned recap module); DMV confined to S6/S7; vending-machine reject, fairground height bar, loyalty punch card, split-flap board, tablecloth-pull each fire once.

## 5. Hero's want — consistent, one continuity break

The through-line holds across all ten: *he wants to do the work and be allowed to do it on his own.* S0 ship it → S1 put it out / get the gun back → S2 drive → S3 be believed, then measure himself → S4 get to the wrench → S5 touch a tool → S6 get graded and choose it (f58, nub flat on the plexi) → S7 earn the licence → S8 clear his own glass → S9 show you the machine. No correction.

- **But his marks do not carry.** S3's hook promises the forehead DONE is "scrubbed off by S5 when he is promoted"; **S4's rookie sprite and S5's manager sprite both omit it.** BINDING: **S4** carries the crooked `#A8B84A` DONE across the forehead and the yellow-green nubs (revealed when the hard hat tips back at f92); **S5** stages its removal at the drafting desk before the f78 pen fires. Absent that, strike S3's hook line and the mark must carry to S8.
- **S8's S2 dependency is wrong-pointed and unbuildable.** S8 demands S2 place the windscreen DONE on camera with a `0.0 m` trip readout — but **S2 bans all numbers**, and the sticker was already placed: it is **S1's sticker 09** (f150, slapped on the panel glass toward camera from the back seat = the windscreen's inside face), which S2 already carries as "Direct S1 artifact." BINDING: strike S8's demanded S2 placement beat and the `0.0 m` readout; re-point the dependency at **S1 f150**; **S8's item 23 must show S2's star-crack under/around the sticker** (same glass), and S8's "six scenes old / S2 planted it" becomes "seven scenes old / S1 planted it."
- **S4 × S5 × S6 — three cards claim to hand S6 the counterfeit it bins.** S4's hook ("the escaped sticker, rejected and binned at S6"), S5's hook ("the fake rides the bundle in, spat out, flicked into the bin"), and S6's own staging (he smooths a note from his own pocket and feeds the slot at f14–52). **RULING: S6's card is canonical.** Strike "(rejected and binned at S6)" from S4's hook — that sticker just leaves the reel. Strike from S5's hook the claim that the fake is fed into S6's intake; S5 hands S6 the **SHUNK and the press coming down**, nothing else (S6's f0–f47 is already assigned to the two jobs being seated).
